.vial-bg-section {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

/* Background graphic layer */
.vial-bg-section::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background-image: url("https://peptira.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/bg-vial.svg");
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    background-position: left top;
    background-size: 45%;
 /* adjust 30–60% to taste */
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 0;
}

/* Make sure your content sits above the background */
.vial-bg-section > * {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
}

.hero-product-wrap {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 420px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    pointer-events: none;
}

.hero-product {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    display: block;
    animation: floatProduct 7s ease-in-out infinite;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 25px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.12));
    transform-origin: center;
}

@keyframes floatProduct {
    0% {
        transform: translateY(0px) scale(1);
    }

    50% {
        transform: translateY(-14px) scale(1.01);
    }

    100% {
        transform: translateY(0px) scale(1);
    }
}

.hero-product-wrap::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    top: -20%;
    left: -60%;
    width: 60%;
    height: 140%;
    background: linear-gradient(
    120deg,
    transparent 0%,
    rgba(255,255,255,0.35) 45%,
    rgba(255,255,255,0.6) 50%,
    rgba(255,255,255,0.35) 55%,
    transparent 100%
  );
    transform: rotate(10deg);
    animation: lightSweep 6s ease-in-out infinite;
    pointer-events: none;
    mix-blend-mode: screen;
}

@keyframes lightSweep {
    0% {
        left: -70%;
        opacity: 0;
    }

    20% {
        opacity: 1;
    }

    50% {
        left: 120%;
        opacity: 1;
    }

    70% {
        opacity: 0;
    }

    100% {
        left: 120%;
        opacity: 0;
    }
}

/* Peptira-style vial background on a specific section */
.vial-bg-section {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

/* Background graphic layer */
.vial-bg-section::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background-image: url("https://peptira.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/bg-vial.svg");
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    background-position: left top;
    background-size: 45%;
 /* adjust 30–60% to taste */
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 0;
}

/* Make sure your content sits above the background */
.vial-bg-section > * {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
}

.hero-product-wrap {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 420px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    pointer-events: none;
}

.hero-product {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    display: block;
    animation: floatProduct 7s ease-in-out infinite;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 25px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.12));
    transform-origin: center;
}

@keyframes floatProduct {
    0% {
        transform: translateY(0px) scale(1);
    }

    50% {
        transform: translateY(-14px) scale(1.01);
    }

    100% {
        transform: translateY(0px) scale(1);
    }
}

.hero-product-wrap::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    top: -20%;
    left: -60%;
    width: 60%;
    height: 140%;
    background: linear-gradient(
    120deg,
    transparent 0%,
    rgba(255,255,255,0.35) 45%,
    rgba(255,255,255,0.6) 50%,
    rgba(255,255,255,0.35) 55%,
    transparent 100%
  );
    transform: rotate(10deg);
    animation: lightSweep 6s ease-in-out infinite;
    pointer-events: none;
    mix-blend-mode: screen;
}

@keyframes lightSweep {
    0% {
        left: -70%;
        opacity: 0;
    }

    20% {
        opacity: 1;
    }

    50% {
        left: 120%;
        opacity: 1;
    }

    70% {
        opacity: 0;
    }

    100% {
        left: 120%;
        opacity: 0;
    }
}

custom-code-c8565G82l8"><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><!----><span></span><div id="custom-code-c8565G82l8" class="custom-code-container ccustom-code-c8565G82l8"><!-- SnapPeptides — Free Shipping Banner (always show, no overlap) -->
<style>
  :root { --sp-teal: #81e6d9FF; }
  #sp-free-ship {
    position: fixed; inset: 0 auto auto 0; width: 100%;
    z-index: 999999;
    background: var(--sp-teal); color: #000;
    font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Arial, sans-serif;
    font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: .2px; line-height: 1;
    box-shadow: 0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,.15);
  }
  #sp-free-ship .sp-inner{
    max-width:1200px;margin:0 auto;padding:10px 56px 10px 16px;
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:42px;
    position:relative;text-align:center;
  }
  #sp-free-ship::before{
    content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none;
    background-image:
      linear-gradient(120deg,rgba(255,255,255,0) 0%,rgba(255,255,255,.10) 20%,rgba(255,255,255,0) 40%),
      repeating-linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(0,0,0,.04) 0 12px,rgba(255,255,255,.04) 12px 24px);
    animation:sp-shimmer 6s linear infinite;
  }
  @keyframes sp-shimmer{0%{background-position:-200% 0,0 0}100%{background-position:200% 0,200px 0}}
  .sp-msg{font-size:clamp(13px,2.6vw,16px);text-transform:uppercase}
  .sp-msg b{animation:sp-pop 2.5s ease-in-out infinite}
  @keyframes sp-pop{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0)}50%{transform:translateY(-1px)}}
  button.sp-close{
    position:absolute;right:8px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);
    width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:999px;border:0;
    background:rgba(0,0,0,.12);color:#000;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;
  }
  button.sp-close:hover{background:rgba(0,0,0,.18)}
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){
    #sp-free-ship::before,.sp-msg b{animation:none}
  }
</style>

<div id="sp-free-ship" role="region" aria-label="Free shipping notice" hidden>
  <div class="sp-inner">
    <div class="sp-msg"><b>Free Shipping</b> on orders <b>$250 or more</b></div>
    <button class="sp-close" aria-label="Dismiss banner" title="Close">&times;</button>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
(function(){
  // Likely GHL header targets (includes your known ID)
  const HEADER_SELECTORS = [
    '#nav-menu-v2-kGQGLU1NVX',
    '.ghl-header', '._builder-header', 'header.site-header'
  ];

  const banner = document.getElementById('sp-free-ship');
  if (!banner) return;

  // Show banner and push headers down so nothing is covered
  function pushDown() {
    const h = banner.getBoundingClientRect().height || 42;
    HEADER_SELECTORS.forEach(sel=>{
      document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(el=>{
        el.style.marginTop = h + 'px';
      });
    });
    // As a fallback, nudge the body only if needed (avoids double-padding)
    document.body.style.scrollMarginTop = h + 'px';
  }

  // Remove offsets when banner is closed
  function resetPush() {
    HEADER_SELECTORS.forEach(sel=>{
      document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(el=>{
        el.style.marginTop = '';
      });
    });
    document.body.style.scrollMarginTop = '';
  }

  // Init: always show on each visit (no local/session storage)
  banner.hidden = false;
  requestAnimationFrame(pushDown);

  // Close only hides for the current page load
  banner.querySelector('.sp-close')?.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    banner.hidden = true;
    resetPush();
  });

  // Recalculate on resize (font/viewport changes)
  let t;
  window.addEventListener('resize', ()=>{
    if (banner.hidden) return;
    clearTimeout(t);
    t = setTimeout(pushDown, 120);
  }, { passive:true });
})();
</script>
<!-- /SnapPeptides — Free Shipping Banner -->

<style>
  @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Merriweather:wght@400;700&display=swap');

  .featurebar-wrap {
    --bg: #f7f7f7;
    --text: #222;
    --muted: #5b6770;
    --heading: #0b63c5;
    --icon: #81e6d9;
    --radius: 18px;

    background: var(--bg);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    padding: 28px 32px;
    max-width: 1200px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    font-family: 'Merriweather', serif;
  }

  .featurebar {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 26px;
    align-items: start;
  }

  @media (min-width: 720px) {
    .featurebar {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
      gap: 36px;
    }
  }

  .feature {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 58px 1fr;
    gap: 16px;
    align-items: start;
  }

  .feature svg {
    width: 46px;
    height: 46px;
    color: var(--icon);
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  .feature h3 {
    margin: 0 0 6px;
    font-size: 17px;
    color: var(--heading);
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .feature p {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 14px;
    line-height: 1.45;
    color: var(--muted);
  }

  @media (max-width: 480px) {
    .featurebar-wrap { padding: 20px 18px; border-radius: 14px; }
    .feature { grid-template-columns: 42px 1fr; gap: 12px; }
    .feature svg { width: 38px; height: 38px; }
    .feature h3 { font-size: 15.5px; }
    .feature p { font-size: 13px; }
  }
</style>

<div class="featurebar-wrap" role="region" aria-label="Store benefits">
  <div class="featurebar">

<style id="snap-agegate-css">
  :root{--teal:#81e6d9;--blk:#0b0b0b;--txt:#f5f5f5}
  .snap-age-overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.75);backdrop-filter:blur(5px);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:99999;
    opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .35s ease}
  .snap-age-overlay.active{opacity:1;pointer-events:auto}
  .snap-age-box{background:rgba(15,15,15,.95);color:var(--txt);width:min(92vw,420px);
    border-radius:16px;border:1px solid var(--teal);box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.6);
    padding:28px 22px;text-align:center}
  .snap-age-box h2{margin:0 0 10px;font:700 22px/1.2 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;color:var(--teal)}
  .snap-age-box p{margin:0 0 18px;font:400 15px/1.6 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;color:#e8e8e8}
  .snap-age-actions{display:flex;gap:10px}
  .snap-age-btn{flex:1;border:0;border-radius:999px;padding:12px 16px;font:700 15px/1 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;cursor:pointer;transition:transform .15s ease,opacity .15s ease}
  .snap-age-btn:active{transform:scale(.98)}
  .snap-age-yes{background:var(--teal);color:#0b0b0b}
  .snap-age-no{background:#242424;color:#f5f5f5;border:1px solid #333}
  html.snap-lock,body.snap-lock{overflow:hidden!important;height:100%!important}
</style>

<script id="snap-agegate-js">
(function(){
  "use strict";
  const KEY="snap_age_verified", DAYS=7, DELAY=10000, NO_URL="https://snappeptides.com/home";

  const safeParse = s => { try { return JSON.parse(s) } catch { return null } };
  const isVerified = () => {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if(!raw) return false;
    const d = safeParse(raw);
    if(!d || !d.ts || d.ok !== true) return false;
    return (Date.now() - d.ts) < (DAYS * 86400000);
  };
  const remember = () => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify({ok:true, ts:Date.now()})); } catch {}
  };

  const buildGate = () => {
    if(document.getElementById("snap-age-overlay")) return;
    const overlay = document.createElement("div");
    overlay.id="snap-age-overlay";
    overlay.className="snap-age-overlay";
    overlay.innerHTML=`
      <div class="snap-age-box" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
        <h2>Age Verification</h2>
        <p>According to FDA guidelines this website requires you to be 21 years or older to enter.<br><br>
        Please confirm your age to continue using Snap Peptides.<br><br>
        Are you over 21 years of age?</p>
        <div class="snap-age-actions">
          <button type="button" class="snap-age-btn snap-age-yes" id="snap-age-yes">Yes</button>
          <button type="button" class="snap-age-btn snap-age-no" id="snap-age-no">No</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    document.documentElement.classList.add("snap-lock");
    document.body.classList.add("snap-lock");
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>overlay.classList.add("active"));

    const yes = document.getElementById("snap-age-yes");
    const no = document.getElementById("snap-age-no");
    yes.onclick = () => {
      remember();
      overlay.classList.remove("active");
      setTimeout(()=>{
        overlay.remove();
        document.documentElement.classList.remove("snap-lock");
        document.body.classList.remove("snap-lock");
      },350);
    };
    no.onclick = () => { window.location.href = NO_URL; };
  };

  const start = () => {
    if(isVerified()) return;
    setTimeout(buildGate, DELAY);
  };

  if(document.readyState === "complete" || document.readyState === "interactive") start();
  else document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", start, {once:true});
})();
</script>

<style>
#header {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    z-index: 10000;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.9);
    backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    transition: all 0.3s ease;
}
body {
    padding-top: 90px;
}
.scrolled #header {
    padding: 6px 0;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.95);
}
</style>
<script>
window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
    if (window.scrollY > 50) {
        document.body.classList.add('scrolled');
    } else {
        document.body.classList.remove('scrolled');
    }
});

repeating-linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(0,0,0,.04) 0 12px,rgba(255,255,255,.04) 12px 24px);\n    animation:sp-shimmer 6s linear infinite;\n  }\n  @keyframes sp-shimmer{0%{background-position:-200% 0,0 aria-label=\"Dismiss banner\" title=\"Close\">&times;\u003C/button>\n  \u003C/div>\n\u003C/div>\n\n\u003Cscript>\n(function(){\n  // Likely GHL header targets (includes your known ID)\n  const HEADER_SELECTORS = [\n    '#nav-menu-v2-kGQGLU1NVX',\n    '.ghl-header', '._builder-header', 'header.site-header'\n  ];\n\n  const banner = document.getElementById('sp-free-ship');\n  if (!banner) return;\n\n  // Show banner and push headers down so nothing is covered\n  function pushDown() {\n    const h = banner.getBoundingClientRect().height || 42;\n    HEADER_SELECTORS.forEach(sel=>{\n      document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(el=>{\n        el.style.marginTop = h + 'px';\n      });\n    });\n    // As a fallback, nudge the body only if needed (avoids double-padding)\n    document.body.style.scrollMarginTop = h + 'px';\n  }\n\n  // Remove offsets when banner is closed\n  function resetPush() {\n    HEADER_SELECTORS.forEach(sel=>{\n      document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(el=>{\n        el.style.marginTop = '';\n      });\n    });\n    document.body.style.scrollMarginTop = '';\n  }\n\n  // Init: always show on each visit (no local/session storage)\n  banner.hidden = false;\n  requestAnimationFrame(pushDown);\n\n  // Close only hides for the current page load\n  banner.querySelector('.sp-close')?.addEventListener('click', ()=>{\n    banner.hidden = true;\n    resetPush();\n  });\n\n  // Recalculate on resize (font/viewport changes)\n  let t;\n  window.addEventListener('resize', ()=>{\n    if (banner.hidden) return;\n    clearTimeout(t);\n    t = setTimeout(pushDown, 120);\n  }, { passive:true });\n})();\n\u003C/script>\n\u003C!-- /SnapPeptides — Free Shipping Banner Snap Peptides Age Gate (7-day memory, 15s delay, transparent) -->\n\u003Cstyle id=\"snap-agegate-css\">\n  :root{--teal:#81e6d9;--blk:#0b0b0b;--txt:#f5f5f5}\n  .snap-age-overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.75);backdrop-filter:blur(5px);\n    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:99999;\n    opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .35s ease}\n  .snap-age-overlay.active{opacity:1;pointer-events:auto}\n  .snap-age-box{background:rgba(15,15,15,.95);color:var(--txt);width:min(92vw,420px);\n    border-radius:16px;border:1px solid var(--teal);box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.6);\n    padding:28px 22px;text-align:center}\n  .snap-age-box h2{margin:0 0 10px;font:700 22px/1.2 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;color:var(--teal)}\n  .snap-age-box p{margin:0 0 18px;font:400 15px/1.6 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;color:#e8e8e8}\n  .snap-age-actions{display:flex;gap:10px}\n  .snap-age-btn{flex:1;border:0;border-radius:999px;padding:12px 16px;font:700 15px/1 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;cursor:pointer;transition:transform .15s ease,opacity .15s ease}\n  .snap-age-btn:active{transform:scale(.98)}\n  .snap-age-yes{background:var(--teal);color:#0b0b0b}\n  .snap-age-no{background:#242424;color:#f5f5f5;border:1px solid #333}\n  html.snap-lock,body.snap-lock{overflow:hidden!important;height:100%!important}\n\u003C/style>\n\n\u003Cscript id=\"snap-agegate-js\">\n(function(){\n  \"use strict\";\n  const KEY=\"snap_age_verified\", DAYS=7, DELAY=10000, NO_URL=\"https://snappeptides.com/home\";\n\n  const safeParse = s => { try { return JSON.parse(s) } catch { return null } };\n  const isVerified = () => {\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);\n    if(!raw) return false;\n    const d = safeParse(raw);\n    if(!d || !d.ts || d.ok !== true) return false;\n    return (Date.now() - d.ts) \u003C (DAYS * 86400000);\n  };\n  const remember = () => {\n    try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify({ok:true, ts:Date.now()})); } catch {}\n  };\n\n  const buildGate = () => {\n    if(document.getElementById(\"snap-age-overlay\")) return;\n    const overlay = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    overlay.id=\"snap-age-overlay\";\n    overlay.className=\"snap-age-overlay\";\n    overlay.innerHTML=`\n      \u003Cdiv class=\"snap-age-box\" role=\"dialog\" aria-modal=\"true\">\n        \u003Ch2>Age Verification\u003C/h2>\n        \u003Cp>According to FDA guidelines this website requires you to be 21 years or older to enter.\u003Cbr>\u003Cbr>\n        Please confirm your age to continue using Snap Peptides.\u003Cbr>\u003Cbr>\n        Are you over 21 years of age?\u003C/p>\n        \u003Cdiv class=\"snap-age-actions\">\n          \u003Cbutton type=\"button\" class=\"snap-age-btn snap-age-yes\" id=\"snap-age-yes\">Yes\u003C/button>\n          \u003Cbutton type=\"button\" class=\"snap-age-btn snap-age-no\" id=\"snap-age-no\">No\u003C/button>\n        \u003C/div>\n      \u003C/div>`;\n    document.body.appendChild(overlay);\n    document.documentElement.classList.add(\"snap-lock\");\n    document.body.classList.add(\"snap-lock\");\n    requestAnimationFrame(()=>overlay.classList.add(\"active\"));\n\n    const yes = document.getElementById(\"snap-age-yes\");\n    const no = document.getElementById(\"snap-age-no\");\n    yes.onclick = () => {\n      remember();\n      overlay.classList.remove(\"active\");\n      setTimeout(()=>{\n        overlay.remove();\n        document.documentElement.classList.remove(\"snap-lock\");\n        document.body.classList.remove(\"snap-lock\");\n      },350);\n    };\n    no.onclick = () => { window.location.href = NO_URL; };\n  };\n\n  const start = () => {\n    if(isVerified()) return;\n    setTimeout(buildGate, DELAY);\n  };\n\n  if(document.readyState === \"complete\" || document.readyState === \"interactive\") start();\n  else document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", start, {once:true});\n})();\n\u003C/script>\n\n\u003Cstyle>\n#header {\n    position: fixed;\n    top: 0;\n    left: 0;\n    width: 100%;\n    z-index: 10000;\n    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.9);\n    backdrop-filter: blur(5px);\n    box-shadow: 0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);\n    transition: all 0.3s ease;\n}\nbody {\n    padding-top: 90px;\n}\n.scrolled #header {\n    padding: 6px 0;\n    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.95);\n}\n\u003C/style>\n\u003Cscript>\nwindow.addEventListener('scroll', function() {\n    if (window.scrollY > 50) {\n        document.body.classList.add('scrolled');\n    } else {\n }\n\n  function findSubmitOrderButton() {\n    const buttons = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(\"button\"));\n    const match = buttons.find(b => normalize(b.textContent) === \"submit order\");\n    return match || null;\n  }\n\n  function injectConsent(btn) {\n    if (!btn) return false;\n    if (document.getElementById(WRAP_ID)) return true;\n\n    const wrap = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    wrap.id = WRAP_ID;\n    wrap.style.cssText =\n      \"margin:14px 0;padding:12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:12px;background:#f8fafc;font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;\";\n\n    wrap.innerHTML = `\n      \u003Clabel style=\"display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;cursor:pointer;\">\n        \u003Cinput id=\"${BOX_ID}\" type=\"checkbox\" style=\"margin-top:3px;transform:scale(1.1);\">\n        \u003Cspan style=\"font-size:13px;line-height:1.35;color:#0f172a;\">\n          I agree to receive order updates and payment reminders by text message and email.\n          Msg & data rates may apply. Reply STOP to opt out.\n          I understand my order is not complete until I finish payment on the next page using the available payment options.\n        \u003C/span>\n      \u003C/label>\n      \u003Cdiv id=\"${ERR_ID}\" style=\"display:none;margin-top:10px;color:#b91c1c;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;\">\n        Please check the box to continue.\n      \u003C/div>\n    `;\n\n    btn.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, btn);\n\n    const box = wrap.querySelector(\"#\" + BOX_ID);\n    const err = wrap.querySelector(\"#\" + ERR_ID);\n\n    function setState() {\n      const ok = !!box.checked;\n      btn.disabled = !ok;\n      btn.style.opacity = ok ? \"1\" : \"0.6\";\n      btn.style.cursor = ok ? \"pointer\" : \"not-allowed\";\n      if (ok) err.style.display = \"none\";\n    }\n\n    setState();\n    box.addEventListener(\"change\", setState);\n\n    // Block click\n    btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function (e) {\n      if (!box.checked) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        e.stopPropagation();\n        err.style.display = \"block\";\n        box.scrollIntoView({ behavior: \"smooth\", block: \"center\" });\n        box.focus();\n        return false;\n      }\n    }, true);\n\n    // Block Enter/programmatic submits if inside a form\n    const form = btn.closest(\"form\");\n    if (form) {\n      form.addEventListener(\"submit\", function (e) {\n        if (!box.checked) {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          e.stopPropagation();\n          err.style.display = \"block\";\n          box.scrollIntoView({ behavior: \"smooth\", block: \"center\" });\n          box.focus();\n          return false;\n        }\n      }, true);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  function tryInject() {\n    const btn = findSubmitOrderButton();\n    if (!btn) return false;\n    return injectConsent(btn);\n  }\n\n  // Try now\n  if (tryInject()) return;\n\n  // Watch DOM for late rendering\n  const obs = new MutationObserver(function () {\n    if (tryInject()) obs.disconnect();\n  });\n\n  obs.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });\n\n  // Stop after 30 seconds\n  setTimeout(() => { try { obs.disconnect(); } catch(e){} }, 30000);\n})();\n\u003C/script>","\u003Cscript>\n(function () {\n  function setNativeValue(element, value) {\n    var valueSetter = \"value\")?.set;\n\n    if (prototypeValueSetter && valueSetter !== prototypeValueSetter) {\n      prototypeValueSetter.call(element, value);\n    } else if (valueSetter) {\n      valueSetter.call(element, value);\n    } else {\n      element.value = value;\n    }\n  }\n\n  function setupSplit(fullNameField) {\n    if (!fullNameField || fullNameField.dataset.splitDone === \"true\") return;\n    fullNameField.dataset.splitDone = \"true\";\n\n    // Hide original field safely without removing it from validation flow\n    fullNameField.style.position = \"absolute\";\n    fullNameField.style.left = \"-9999px\";\n    fullNameField.style.width = \"1px\";\n    fullNameField.style.height = \"1px\";\n    fullNameField.style.opacity = \"0\";\n    fullNameField.style.pointerEvents = \"none\";\n\n    // Optional: if GHL already has a value, split it\n    var existingValue = (fullNameField.value || \"\").trim();\n    var firstName = \"\";\n    var lastName = \"\";\n\n    if (existingValue) {\n      var parts = existingValue.split(\" \");\n      firstName = parts.shift() || \"\";\n      lastName = parts.join(\" \");\n    }\n\n    var wrapper = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    wrapper.style.display = \"flex\";\n    wrapper.style.flexWrap = \"wrap\";\n    wrapper.style.gap = \"10px\";\n    wrapper.style.marginBottom = \"10px\";\n\n    var first = document.createElement(\"input\");\n    first.type = \"text\";\n    first.placeholder = \"First Name\";\n    first.className = fullNameField.className;\n    first.value = firstName;\n    first.autocomplete = \"given-name\";\n    first.style.flex = \"1 1 160px\";\n\n    var last = document.createElement(\"input\");\n    last.type = \"text\";\n    last.placeholder = \"Last Name\";\n    last.className = fullNameField.className;\n    last.value = lastName;\n    last.autocomplete = \"family-name\";\n    last.style.flex = \"1 1 160px\";\n\n    fullNameField.parentNode.insertBefore(wrapper, fullNameField);\n    wrapper.appendChild(first);\n    wrapper.appendChild(last);\n\n    function updateFull() {\n      var full = (first.value + \" \" + last.value).replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim();\n\n      setNativeValue(fullNameField, full);\n\n      fullNameField.dispatchEvent(new Event(\"input\", { bubbles: true }));\n      fullNameField.dispatchEvent(new Event(\"change\", { bubbles: true }));\n      fullNameField.dispatchEvent(new Event(\"blur\", { bubbles: true }));\n    }\n\n    first.addEventListener(\"input\", updateFull);\n    last.addEventListener(\"input\", updateFull);\n    first.addEventListener(\"blur\", updateFull);\n    last.addEventListener(\"blur\", updateFull);\n\n    updateFull();\n  }\n\n  function findAndSetup() {\n    var field =\n      document.querySelector('input.form-input.input.hl-checkout-input[name=\"name\"]') ||\n      document.querySelector('input[name=\"name\"]');\n\n    if (field) setupSplit(field);\n  }\n\n  window.addEventListener(\"load\", function () {\n    setTimeout(findAndSetup, 800);\n  });\n\n  var observer = new MutationObserver(function () {\n    var field =\n      document.querySelector('input.form-input.input.hl-checkout-input[name=\"name\"]') ||\n      document.querySelector('input[name=\"name\"]');\n\n    if (field) {\n      setupSplit(field);\n    }\n  });\n\n  observer.observe(document.documentElement, {\n    childList: true,\n    subtree: true\n  });\n})();\n\u003C/script>","jLaiJFsb5zxlIA1QN23h","Snap
